import numpy as np
from collections import Counter, deque

for i, ex in enumerate(train):
    g = np.array(ex['input'])
    o = np.array(ex['output'])
    print('=== ex', i, 'shape', g.shape)
    vals = Counter(g.flatten().tolist())
    bg = vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    print('bg', bg, 'counts', dict(vals))
    # objects by color
    for col in sorted(set(g.flatten().tolist())):
        if col==bg: continue
        cells = list(zip(*np.where(g==col)))
        print(' color', col, 'n', len(cells), 'cells', cells)
    print(' output colors:')
    for col in sorted(set(o.flatten().tolist())):
        if col==bg: continue
        cells = list(zip(*np.where(o==col)))
        print(' color', col, 'n', len(cells), 'cells', cells)

[TOOL CALL run_python]

import numpy as np, itertools

def shapes_cells(cells):
    return set(cells)

def transforms():
    # 8 dihedral on (r,c)
    ts = []
    for k in range(4):
        ts.append(lambda r,c,k=k: (r,c) if k==0 else (c,-r) if k==1 else (-r,-c) if k==2 else (-c,r))
    # reflections
    ts2 = []
    for f in [lambda r,c:(r,-c), lambda r,c:(-r,c), lambda r,c:(c,r), lambda r,c:(-c,-r)]:
        ts2.append(f)
    allt = []
    for k in range(4):
        if k==0: allt.append(lambda r,c:(r,c))
        if k==1: allt.append(lambda r,c:(c,-r))
        if k==2: allt.append(lambda r,c:(-r,-c))
        if k==3: allt.append(lambda r,c:(-c,r))
    allt += [lambda r,c:(r,-c), lambda r,c:(-r,c), lambda r,c:(c,r), lambda r,c:(-c,-r)]
    return allt

def check_isometry(A, B, fixed_cells):
    # A,B sets of (r,c). find transform T and translation so T(A) approx = B, extra cells subset of fixed_cells
    Aset=set(A); Bset=set(B)
    for T in transforms():
        TA=[T(r,c) for (r,c) in Aset]
        # try translation to align: choose d such that bounding min aligns? brute force d
        # candidate d = (b - ta) for some pairs
        for (pr,pc) in Bset:
            # try anchor TA[0] to pr,pc
            pass
        # simpler: for each anchor in TA and B, set d
        for ta in TA:
            for b in Bset:
                d=(b[0]-ta[0], b[1]-ta[1])
                mapped=set((r+d[0],c+d[1]) for r,c in TA)
                if mapped==Bset:
                    return T, d, 'exact'
                if Bset <= mapped and mapped-Bset <= set(fixed_cells):
                    return T,d,'subset'
    return None

for i, ex in enumerate(train):
    g = np.array(ex['input']); o = np.array(ex['output'])
    vals = Counter(g.flatten().tolist())
    bg = vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    # fixed colors = those whose cells identical between in and out
    fixed=set()
    mov=None
    for col in set(g.flatten().tolist())|set(o.flatten().tolist()):
        if col==bg: continue
        ci=set(zip(*np.where(g==col))); co=set(zip(*np.where(o==col)))
        if ci==co: fixed|=co
        else: mov=col; mi=ci; mo=co
    fixed_cells=fixed
    res=check_isometry(mi,mo,fixed_cells)
    print('ex',i,'moved color',mov,'result',res)

def render(cells):
    rs=[r for r,c in cells]; cs=[c for r,c in cells]
    r0,r1=min(rs),max(rs); c0,c1=min(cs),max(cs)
    g=[['.']*(c1-c0+1) for _ in range(r1-r0+1)]
    for r,c in cells: g[r-r0][c-c0]='#'
    return '\n'.join(''.join(row) for row in g)

for i, ex in enumerate(train):
    g = np.array(ex['input']); o = np.array(ex['output'])
    vals=Counter(g.flatten().tolist()); bg=vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    print('=== ex',i)
    for col in sorted(set(g.flatten().tolist())|set(o.flatten().tolist())):
        if col==bg: continue
        ci=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(g==col)])); co=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(o==col)]))
        print(' color',col,'IN:'); print(render(ci)); print(' OUT:'); print(render(co))

def all_transforms():
    allt = []
    allt.append(lambda r,c:(r,c))
    allt.append(lambda r,c:(c,-r))
    allt.append(lambda r,c:(-r,-c))
    allt.append(lambda r,c:(-c,r))
    allt.append(lambda r,c:(r,-c))
    allt.append(lambda r,c:(-r,c))
    allt.append(lambda r,c:(c,r))
    allt.append(lambda r,c:(-c,-r))
    return allt

for i, ex in enumerate(train):
    g=np.array(ex['input']);o=np.array(ex['output'])
    vals=Counter(g.flatten().tolist());bg=vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    mov=None
    for col in set(g.flatten().tolist())|set(o.flatten().tolist()):
        if col==bg: continue
        ci=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(g==col)]));co=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(o==col)]))
        if ci!=co: mov=(col,ci,co)
    col,ci,co=mov
    found=[]
    for T in all_transforms():
        TA=[T(r,c) for r,c in ci]
        for ta in TA:
            for b in co:
                d=(b[0]-ta[0],b[1]-ta[1])
                mapped=set((r+d[0],c+d[1]) for r,c in TA)
                if co<=mapped:
                    found.append(('sub',d))
    print('ex',i,'moved',col,'#in',len(ci),'#out',len(co),'subset isometries:',found[:3],'count',len(found))

[TOOL CALL run_python]

trans=[lambda r,c:(r,c),lambda r,c:(c,-r),lambda r,c:(-r,-c),lambda r,c:(-c,r),
       lambda r,c:(r,-c),lambda r,c:(-r,c),lambda r,c:(c,r),lambda r,c:(-c,-r)]
for i,ex in enumerate(train):
    g=np.array(ex['input']);o=np.array(ex['output'])
    vals=Counter(g.flatten().tolist());bg=vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    fixed=set();mov=None
    for col in set(g.flatten().tolist())|set(o.flatten().tolist()):
        if col==bg:continue
        ci=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(g==col)]));co=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(o==col)]))
        if ci==co: fixed|=co
        else: mov=(col,ci,co)
    col,ci,co=mov
    cand=co|fixed
    ok=[]
    for ti,T in enumerate(trans):
        TA=[T(r,c) for r,c in ci]
        for ta in TA:
            for b in cand:
                d=(b[0]-ta[0],b[1]-ta[1])
                mapped=set((r+d[0],c+d[1]) for r,c in TA)
                if mapped==cand or (co<=mapped and mapped<=cand):
                    ok.append((ti,d))
    print('ex',i,'ok',ok)

[TOOL CALL run_python]

import numpy as np
from collections import Counter
def order_path(cells, start):
    cells=set(cells); 
    # find path visiting all (assume exists). do DFS
    def dfs(cur, path, remaining):
        if not remaining: return path
        r,c=cur
        for dr in (-1,0,1):
            for dc in (-1,0,1):
                if dr==0 and dc==0: continue
                n=(r+dr,c+dc)
                if n in remaining:
                    res=dfs(n,path+[n],remaining-{n})
                    if res: return res
        return None
    return dfs(start,[start],cells-{start})

def dname(a,b):
    dr=b[0]-a[0]; dc=b[1]-a[1]
    return {(1,0):'D',(-1,0):'U',(0,1):'R',(0,-1):'L',
            (1,1):'DR',(1,-1):'DL',(-1,1):'UR',(-1,-1):'UL'}[(dr,dc)]

for i,ex in enumerate(train):
    g=np.array(ex['input']);o=np.array(ex['output'])
    vals=Counter(g.flatten().tolist());bg=vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    fixed=set();mov=None
    for col in set(g.flatten().tolist())|set(o.flatten().tolist()):
        if col==bg:continue
        ci=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(g==col)]));co=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(o==col)]))
        if ci==co: fixed|=co
        else: mov=(col,ci,co)
    col,ci,co=mov
    # find anchor: moved cell adjacent to a fixed cell
    def anchor(cells):
        for p in cells:
            for q in fixed:
                if abs(p[0]-q[0])<=1 and abs(p[1]-q[1])<=1:
                    return p
        return None
    ai=anchor(ci); ao=anchor(co)
    pi=order_path(ci,ai); po=order_path(co,ao)
    di=[dname(pi[k],pi[k+1]) for k in range(len(pi)-1)] if pi else None
    do=[dname(po[k],po[k+1]) for k in range(len(po)-1)] if po else None
    print('ex',i,'anchor in',ai,'out',ao)
    print('  in dirs',di)

[TOOL CALL run_python]

import numpy as np
from collections import Counter
def ident(ex):
    g=np.array(ex['input']);o=np.array(ex['output'])
    vals=Counter(g.flatten().tolist());bg=vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    fixed=set();mov=None
    for col in set(g.flatten().tolist())|set(o.flatten().tolist()):
        if col==bg:continue
        ci=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(g==col)]));co=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(o==col)]))
        if ci==co: fixed|=co
        else: mov=(col,ci,co)
    return bg,fixed,mov

for i,ex in enumerate(train):
    bg,fixed,(col,ci,co)=ident(ex)
    # anchor: moved cell adjacent to a fixed cell that is part of the largest straight line
    # just find moved cell adjacent to any fixed cell
    anch=None
    for p in ci:
        if any(abs(p[0]-q[0])<=1 and abs(p[1]-q[1])<=1 for q in fixed):
            anch=p;break
    # nearest fixed cell
    q=min(fixed,key=lambda q:max(abs(q[0]-anch[0]),abs(q[1]-anch[1])))
    d=np.array([q[0]-anch[0],q[1]-anch[1]],float)
    dd=d/d.dot(d)
    P=2*np.outer(d,dd)-np.eye(2)
    mapped=set()
    for p in ci:
        v=np.array([p[0]-anch[0],p[1]-anch[1]],float)
        w=P.dot(v)
        mapped.add((int(round(w[0]))+anch[0], int(round(w[1]))+anch[1]))
    outrel=set((p[0]-anch[0],p[1]-anch[1]) for p in co)
    print('ex',i,'anchor',anch,'nearest small',tuple(q),'reflection match', mapped==outrel)
    print('  refl',sorted(mapped),' out',sorted(outrel))

import numpy as np
from collections import Counter
def info(ex):
    g=np.array(ex['input']);o=np.array(ex['output'])
    vals=Counter(g.flatten().tolist());bg=vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    fixed=set();mov=None
    for col in set(g.flatten().tolist())|set(o.flatten().tolist()):
        if col==bg:continue
        ci=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(g==col)]));co=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(o==col)]))
        if ci==co: fixed|=co
        else: mov=(col,ci,co)
    return bg,fixed,mov
def order_path(cells, start):
    cells=set(cells)
    def dfs(cur, path, rem):
        if not rem: return path
        r,c=cur
        for dr in (-1,0,1):
            for dc in (-1,0,1):
                if dr==0 and dc==0: continue
                n=(r+dr,c+dc)
                if n in rem:
                    res=dfs(n,path+[n],rem-{n})
                    if res: return res
        return None
    return dfs(start,[start],cells-{start})
for i,ex in enumerate(train):
    bg,fixed,(col,ci,co)=info(ex)
    # anchor: moved cell adjacent to a LONG straight line among fixed
    # find largest straight component in fixed
    # simple: anchor = moved cell adjacent to max number of fixed? use adjacency to any fixed
    def anch(cells):
        best=None
        for p in cells:
            cnt=sum(1 for q in fixed if max(abs(p[0]-q[0]),abs(p[1]-q[1]))<=1)
            if cnt>0:
                return p
        return None
    ai=anch(ci); ao=anch(co)
    pi=order_path(ci,ai); po=order_path(co,ao)
    # pair by index from anchor
    n=min(len(pi),len(po))
    A=np.array([[pi[k][0]-ai[0],pi[k][1]-ai[1]] for k in range(n)],float)
    B=np.array([[po[k][0]-ao[0],po[k][1]-ao[1]] for k in range(n)],float)
    # solve B = A @ M^T  => M^T = lstsq(A,B)
    M,res,rk,sv=np.linalg.lstsq(A,B,rcond=None)
    pred=A@M
    err=np.abs(pred-B).max()
    print('ex',i,'n',n,'M=',np.round(M,3).tolist(),'maxerr',round(float(err),3))

import numpy as np
from collections import Counter
def info(ex):
    g=np.array(ex['input']);o=np.array(ex['output'])
    vals=Counter(g.flatten().tolist());bg=vals.most_common(1)[0][0]
    fixed=set();mov=None
    for col in set(g.flatten().tolist())|set(o.flatten().tolist()):
        if col==bg:continue
        ci=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(g==col)]));co=set(zip(*[a.tolist() for a in np.where(o==col)]))
        if ci==co: fixed|=co
        else: mov=(col,ci,co)
    return bg,fixed,mov
def bres(a,b):
    # 8-connected line from a to b
    (r0,c0),(r1,c1)=a,b
    dr=r1-r0; dc=c1-c0
    n=max(abs(dr),abs(dc))
    if n==0: return [a]
    pts=[]
    for k in range(n+1):
        rr=r0+round(dr*k/n); cc=c0+round(dc*k/n)
        pts.append((rr,cc))
    return pts
for i,ex in enumerate(train):
    bg,fixed,(col,ci,co)=info(ex)
    # identify line: largest set of collinear fixed cells
    # anchor: moved cell adjacent to a fixed cell
    bestline=None
    # group fixed by color
    gc=Counter()

    # just find moved cell adjacent to fixed with max neighbors
    ai=None
    for p in ci:
        for q in fixed:
            if max(abs(p[0]-q[0]),abs(p[1]-q[1]))<=1:

        if ai:break
